{"type":"assistant","message":{"id":"05c78626e9ac7e8c3e35d874ce82ce7f","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"call_function_tyj5x3xcndsf_1","name":"Max","input":{"file_path":"/root/dispatch_solution.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nGrid Dispatch Operator - Economic Dispatch with Spinning Reserves\nSolves for optimal generator dispatch considering:\n1. DC power balance at each bus\n2. Generator and transmission limits\n3. Spinning reserve requirements with standard capacity coupling\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nfrom scipy.optimize import minimize\nfrom typing import Dict, List, Tuple\n\ndef load_network(filepath: str) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Load MATPOWER format network data\"\"\"\n    with open(filepath, 'r') as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n    return data\n\ndef parse_bus_data(bus_data: List) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Parse bus data from MATPOWER format\"\"\"\n    buses = {}\n    for bus in bus_data:\n        bus_id = int(bus[0])\n        buses[bus_id] = {\n            'Pd': bus[3],   # Active power demand (MW)\n            'Qd': bus[4],   # Reactive power demand\n            'Gs': bus[5],   # Sh conductance\n            'Bs': bus[6],   # Sh susceptance\n        }\n    return buses\n\ndef parse_gen_data(gen_data: List) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Parse generator data from MATPOWER format\"\"\"\n    generators = {}\n    for idx, gen in enumerate(gen_data):\n        bus_id = int(gen[0])\n        generators[idx] = {\n            'bus': bus_id,\n            'Pg': gen[1],    # Current active power output\n            'Qg': gen[2],    # Current reactive power output\n            'Qmax': gen[3],  # Max reactive power\n            'Qmin': gen[4],  # Min reactive power\n            'Vg': gen[5],    # Voltage magnitude setpoint\n            'mBase': gen[6], # Machine MVA base\n            'gen_status': int(gen[7]), # 1=in service\n            'Pmax': gen[8],  # Max active power (MW)\n            'Pmin': gen[9],  # Min active power (MW)\n        }\n    return generators\n\ndef parse_gencost_data(gencost_data: List) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Parse generator cost data (polynomial format)\"\"\"\n    costs = {}\n    for idx, cost in enumerate(gencost_data):\n        model = int(cost[0])\n        startup = cost[1]\n        shutdown = cost[2]\n        ncost = int(cost[3])\n\n        # Extract cost coefficients (highest order first)\n        coeffs = cost[4:4+ncost]\n\n        costs[idx] = {\n            'model': model,\n            'startup': startup,\n            'shutdown': shutdown,\n            'ncost': ncost,\n            'coeffs': coeffs\n        }\n    return costs\n\ndef parse_branch_data(branch_data: List) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Parse branch (transmission line) data from MATPOWER format\"\"\"\n    branches = {}\n    for idx, branch in enumerate(branch_data):\n        branches[idx] = {\n            'from_bus': int(branch[0]),\n            'to_bus': int(branch[1]),\n            'r': branch[2],      # Resistance (pu)\n            'x': branch[3],     # Reactance (pu)\n            'b': branch[4],     # Line charging susceptance (pu)\n            'rateA': branch[5], # Long term rating (MVA)\n            'rateB': branch[6],  # Intermediate term rating (MVA)\n            'rateC': branch[7], # Short term rating (MVA)\n            'angle_min': branch[10], # Min angle difference (deg)\n            'angle_max': branch[11], # Max angle difference (deg)\n            'status': int(branch[9])  # 1=in service\n        }\n    return branches\n\ndef build_y_matrix(branches: Dict, buses: Dict) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:\n    \"\"\"Build bus admittance matrix for DC power flow\"\"\"\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    Y = np.zeros((n_buses, n_buses), dtype=complex)\n\n    for idx, branch in branches.items():\n        if branch['status'] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        from_bus_idx = branch['from_bus'] - 1\n        to_bus_idx = branch['to_bus'] - 1\n\n        # For DC power flow, we use only reactance\n        x = branch['x']\n        if abs(x) < 1e-10:\n            continue\n\n        # Series admittance\n        y = 1.0j / x\n\n        # Add to Y matrix\n        Y[from_bus_idx, from_bus_idx] += y\n        Y[to_bus_idx, to_bus_idx] += y\n        Y[from_bus_idx, to_bus_idx] -= y\n        Y[to_bus_idx, from_bus_idx] -= y\n\n    return Y\n\ndef get_cost_function(cost_data: Dict) -> callable:\n    \"\"\"Create cost function from cost coefficients\"\"\"\n    coeffs = cost_data['coeffs']\n\n    # MATPOWER uses: cost = an*P^2 + bn*P + cn\n    # coeffs are ordered from highest to lowest degree\n    if len(coeffs) == 3:\n        an, bn, cn = coeffs\n    elif len(coeffs) == 2:\n        an, bn = coeffs\n        cn = 0.0\n    elif len(coeffs) == 1:\n        an, bn = 0.0, coeffs[0]\n        cn = 0.0\n    else:\n        an = coeffs[0] if len(coeffs) > 0 else 0.0\n        bn = coeffs[1] if len(coeffs) > 1 else 0.0\n        cn = coeffs[2] if len(coeffs) > 2 else 0.0\n\n    def cost_func(p):\n        # p is vector of [Pg, Rg] for each generator\n        n_gens = len(p) // 2\n        total_cost = 0.0\n        for i in range(n_gens):\n            pg = p[i]\n            total_cost += an * pg**2 + bn * pg + cn\n        return total_cost\n\n    return cost_func, (an, bn)\n\ndef solve_economic_dispatch(network_data: Dict, reserve_percentage: float = 0.05) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve economic dispatch with spinning reserves using optimization\n\n    reserve_percentage: Spinning reserve requirement as percentage of load\n    \"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network_data['baseMVA']\n    buses = parse_bus_data(network_data['bus'])\n    generators = parse_gen_data(network_data['gen'])\n    gencosts = parse_gencost_data(network_data['gencost'])\n    branches = parse_branch_data(network_data['branch'])\n\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n\n    # Calculate total load\n    total_load = sum(bus['Pd'] for bus in buses.values())\n    reserve_requirement = reserve_percentage * total_load\n\n    # Prepare optimization variables\n    # Variables: [Pg1, Pg2, ..., Pgn, R1, R2, ..., Rn] for each generator\n    n_vars = 2 * n_gens\n\n    # Initial guess: start from current generation or midpoint of range\n    x0 = []\n    bounds = []\n    reserve_bounds = []\n\n    # Create mapping from generator index to variable index\n    gen_to_var = {}\n\n    for gen_idx in range(n_gens):\n        gen = generators[gen_idx]\n        if gen['gen_status'] == 0:\n            # Generator offline\n            x0.append(0.0)\n            bounds.append((0.0, 0.0))\n            gen_to_var[gen_idx] = {'pg': None, 'reserve': None}\n        else:\n            # Active generator\n            pg0 = gen['Pg'] if gen['Pg'] > 0 else (gen['Pmin'] + gen['Pmax']) / 2\n            x0.append(pg0)\n            bounds.append((gen['Pmin'], gen['Pmax']))\n\n            # Reserve is capacity coupling: reserve <= Pmax - Pg\n            max_reserve = gen['Pmax'] - pg0\n            x0.append(0.0)  # Initial reserve\n            bounds.append((0.0, max_reserve))\n\n            gen_to_var[gen_idx] = {\n                'pg': gen_idx,\n                'reserve': n_gens + gen_idx\n            }\n\n    # Build power balance constraint\n    # Sum of generation must equal load (plus losses approximated as 0 for DC power flow)\n    A_eq = np.zeros(n_vars)\n    for gen_idx in range(n_gens):\n        var_mapping = gen_to_var[gen_idx]\n        if var_mapping['pg'] is not None:\n            A_eq[var_mapping['pg']] = 1.0\n\n    b_eq = [total_load]\n\n    # Build reserve requirement constraint\n    A_neq = np.zeros(n_vars)\n    for gen_idx in range(n_gens):\n        var_mapping = gen_to_var[gen_idx]\n        if var_mapping['reserve'] is not None:\n            A_neq[var_mapping['reserve']] = 1.0\n\n    b_ineq = [reserve_requirement]\n\n    # Transmission constraints (simplified - we'll enforce in post-processing)\n    # For now, focus on generation dispatch with reserves\n\n    # Objective function\n    total_cost_coeffs = []\n    for gen_idx in range(n_gens):\n        if gen_idx in gencosts:\n            coeffs = gencosts[gen_idx]['coeffs']\n            if len(coeffs) == 3:\n                total_cost_coeffs.extend(coeffs)\n            elif len(coeffs) == 2:\n                total_cost_coeffs.extend([0.0] + coeffs)\n            else:\n                total_cost_coeffs.extend([0.0, 0.0] + coeffs)\n\n    def objective(x):\n        cost = 0.0\n        for gen_idx in range(n_gens):\n            if gen_idx in gencosts and gen_to_var[gen_idx]['pg'] is not None:\n                pg_idx = gen_to_var[gen_idx]['pg']\n                pg = x[pg_idx]\n                coeffs = gencosts[gen_idx]['coeffs']\n                if len(coeffs) == 3:\n                    cost += coeffs[0] * pg**2 + coeffs[1] * pg + coeffs[2]\n                elif len(coeffs) == 2:\n                    cost += coeffs[0] * pg + coeffs[1]\n                elif len(coeffs) == 1:\n                    cost += coeffs[0] * pg\n        return cost\n\n    # Constraints\n    constraints = [\n        {'type': 'eq', 'fun': lambda x: A_eq @ x - b_eq[0]},\n        {'type': 'ineq', 'fun': lambda x: b_ineq[0] - A_neq @ x}\n    ]\n\n    # Bounds already defined\n\n    # Solve optimization\n    print(f\"Solving optimization for {n_gens} generators, {n_buses} buses...\")\n    print(f\"Total load: {total_load:.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"Reserve requirement: {reserve_requirement:.2f} MW\")\n\n    result = minimize(\n        objective,\n        x0,\n        method='SLSQP',\n        bounds=bounds,\n        constraints=constraints,\n        options={'maxiter': 1000, 'ftol': 1e-9}\n    )\n\n    if not result.success:\n        print(f\"Warning: Optimization did not converge: {result.message}\")\n\n    # Extract results\n    dispatch_results = []\n    total_generation = 0.0\n    total_reserves = 0.0\n    total_cost = objective(result.x)\n\n    for gen_idx in range(n_gens):\n        gen = generators[gen_idx]\n        if gen_to_var[gen_idx]['pg'] is not None:\n            pg_idx = gen_to_var[gen_idx]['pg']\n            reserve_idx = gen_to_var[gen_idx]['reserve']\n\n            pg = result.x[pg_idx]\n            reserve = result.x[reserve_idx]\n\n            dispatch_results.append({\n                'id': gen_idx,\n                'bus': gen['bus'],\n                'output_MW': pg,\n                'reserve_MW': reserve,\n                'pmax_MW': gen['Pmax']\n            })\n\n            total_generation += pg\n            total_reserves += reserve\n        else:\n            # Offline generator\n            dispatch_results.append({\n                'id': gen_idx,\n                'bus': gen['bus'],\n                'output_MW': 0.0,\n                'reserve_MW': 0.0,\n                'pmax_MW': gen['Pmax']\n            })\n\n    # Calculate line flows using DC power flow\n    line_flows = calculate_line_flows(result.x, generators, branches, buses, baseMVA)\n\n    # Get most loaded lines\n    most_loaded = sorted(line_flows, key=lambda x: abs(x['loading_pct']), reverse=True)[:3]\n\n    # Calculate operating margin\n    total_capacity = sum(gen['Pmax'] for gen in generators.values() if gen['gen_status'] == 1)\n    operating_margin = total_capacity - total_generation - total_reserves\n\n    return {\n        'generator_dispatch': dispatch_results,\n        'totals': {\n            'cost_dollars_per_hour': total_cost,\n            'load_MW': total_load,\n            'generation_MW': total_generation,\n            'reserve_MW': total_reserves\n        },\n        'most_loaded_lines': most_loaded,\n        'operating_margin_MW': operating_margin\n    }\n\ndef calculate_line_flows(x: np.ndarray, generators: Dict, branches: Dict,\n                        buses: Dict, baseMVA: float) -> List[Dict]:\n    \"\"\"Calculate transmission line flows using DC power flow\"\"\"\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n\n    # Build admittance matrix\n    Y = build_y_matrix(branches, buses)\n\n    # Get generator outputs\n    pg_values = np.zeros(n_buses)\n    for gen_idx in range(n_gens):\n        gen = generators[gen_idx]\n        if gen['gen_status'] == 1:\n            pg_values[gen['bus'] - 1] = x[gen_idx]\n\n    # Build injection vector (generation - demand)\n    bus_injections = np.zeros(n_buses)\n    for bus_id, bus_data in buses.items():\n        bus_idx = bus_id - 1\n        bus_injections[bus_idx] = pg_values[bus_idx] - bus_data['Pd']\n\n    # Solve for voltage angles using DC power flow approximation\n    # B * theta = P_inj (for DC power flow, we use the imaginary part)\n    # For DC power flow, we approximate with the reactance matrix\n\n    # Build susceptance matrix (imaginary part of Y)\n    B = np.zeros((n_buses, n_buses))\n    for i in range(n_buses):\n        for j in range(n_buses):\n            if i != j:\n                B[i, j] = -Y[i, j].imag\n            else:\n                B[i, j] = sum(Y[i, k].imag for k in range(n_buses) if k != i)\n\n    # Reference bus is typically bus 1, remove it from the system\n    # For simplicity, assume bus 1 is the reference\n    B_reduced = B[1:, 1:]\n    P_reduced = bus_injections[1:]\n\n    try:\n        theta_reduced = np.linalg.solve(B_reduced, P_reduced)\n        theta = np.zeros(n_buses)\n        theta[1:] = theta_reduced\n        theta[0] = 0.0  # Reference bus\n    except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n        # If singular, use pseudo-inverse\n        theta = np.linalg.pinv(B) @ bus_injections\n        theta[0] = 0.0\n\n    # Calculate line flows\n    line_flows = []\n    for idx, branch in branches.items():\n        if branch['status'] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        from_bus = branch['from_bus'] - 1\n        to_bus = branch['to_bus'] - 1\n\n        # Power flow: P = (theta_from - theta_to) / x\n        x = branch['x']\n        if abs(x) > 1e-10:\n            flow = (theta[from_bus] - theta[to_bus]) / x\n        else:\n            flow = 0.0\n\n        # Calculate loading percentage\n        rating = branch['rateA'] if branch['rateA'] > 0 else 999999\n        loading_pct = abs(flow / rating) * 100\n\n        line_flows.append({\n            'from': branch['from_bus'],\n            'to': branch['to_bus'],\n            'loading_pct': loading_pct,\n            'flow_MW': abs(flow),\n            'rating_MVA': rating\n        })\n\n    return line_flows\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution\"\"\"\n    # Load network data\n    print(\"Loading network data...\")\n    network_data = load_network('/root/network.json')\n\n    # Solve economic dispatch\n    print(\"\\nSolving economic dispatch...\")\n    results = solve_economic_dispatch(network_data, reserve_percentage=0.05)\n\n    # DC power balance at each bus\n2. Generator and transmission limits\n3. Spinning reserve requirements with standard capacity coupling\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nfrom scipy.optimize import minimize\nfrom typing import Dict, List, Tuple\n\ndef load_network(filepath: str) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Load MATPOWER format network data\"\"\"\n    with open(filepath, 'r') as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n    return data\n\ndef parse_bus_data(bus_data: List) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Parse bus data from MATPOWER format\"\"\"\n    buses = {}\n    for bus in bus_data:\n        bus_id = int(bus[0])\n        buses[bus_id] = {\n            'Pd': bus[3],   # Active power demand (MW)\n            'Qd': bus[4],   # Reactive power demand\n            'Gs': bus[5],   # Sh conductance\n            'Bs': bus[6],   # Sh susceptance\n        }\n    return buses\n\ndef parse_gen_data(gen_data: List) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Parse generator data from MATPOWER format\"\"\"\n    generators = {}\n    for idx, gen in enumerate(gen_data):\n        bus_id = int(gen[0])\n        generators[idx] = {\n            'bus': bus_id,\n            'Pg': gen[1],    # Current active power output\n            'Qg': gen[2],    # Current reactive power output\n            'Qmax': gen[3],  # Max reactive power\n            'Qmin': gen[4],  # Min reactive power\n            'Vg': gen[5],    # Extract cost coefficients (highest order first)\n        coeffs = cost[4:4+ncost]\n\n        costs[idx] = {\n            'model': model,\n            'startup': startup,\n            'shutdown': shutdown,\n            'ncost': ncost,\n            'coeffs': coeffs\n        }\n    return costs\n\ndef parse_branch_data(branch_data: List) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Parse branch (transmission line) data from MATPOWER format\"\"\"\n    branches = {}\n    for idx, branch in enumerate(branch_data):\n        branches[idx] = {\n            'from_bus': int(branch[0]),\n            'to_bus': int(branch[1]),\n            'r': branch[2],      # Resistance (pu)\n            'x': branch[3],     # Reactance (pu)\n            'b': branch[4],     # Line charging susceptance (pu)\n            'rateA': branch[5], # Long term rating (MVA)\n            'rateB': branch[6],  # Intermediate term rating (MVA)\n            'rateC': branch[7],  # Short term rating (MVA)\n            'angle_min': branch[10], # Min angle difference (deg)\n            'angle_max': branch[11], # Max angle difference (deg)\n            'status': int(branch[9])  # 1=in service\n        }\n    return branches\n\ndef build_y_matrix(branches: Dict, buses: Dict) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:\n    \"\"\"Build bus admittance matrix for DC power flow\"\"\"\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    Y = np.zeros((n_buses, n_buses), dtype=complex)\n\n    for idx, branch in branches.items():\n        if branch['status'] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        from_bus_idx = branch['from_bus'] - 1\n        to_bus_idx = branch['to_bus'] - 1\n\n        # For DC power flow, we use only reactance\n        x = branch['x']\n        if abs(x) < 1e-10:\n            continue\n\n        # Series admittance\n        y = 1.0j / x\n\n        # Add to Y matrix\n        Y[from_bus_idx, from_bus_idx] += y\n        Y[to_bus_idx, to_bus_idx] += y\n        Y[from_bus_idx, to_bus_idx] -= y\n        Y[to_bus_idx, from_bus_idx] -= y\n\n    return Y\n\ndef solve_economic_dispatch(network_data: Dict, reserve_percentage: float = 0.05) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve economic dispatch with spinning reserves using optimization\n\n    reserve_percentage: Spinning reserve requirement as percentage of load\n    \"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network_data['baseMVA']\n    buses = parse_bus_data(network_data['bus'])\n    generators = parse_gen_data(network_data['gen'])\n    gencosts = parse_gencost_data(network_data['gencost'])\n    branches = parse_branch_data(network_data['branch'])\n\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n\n    # Calculate total load\n    total_load = sum(bus['Pd'] for bus in buses.values())\n    reserve_requirement = reserve_percentage * total_load\n\n    # Prepare optimization variables\n    # Variables: [Pg1, Pg2, ..., Pgn, R1, R2, ..., Rn] for each generator\n    n_vars = 2 * n_gens\n\n    # Initial guess: start from current generation or midpoint of range\n    x0 = []\n    bounds = []\n\n    # Create mapping from generator index to variable index\n    gen_to_var = {}\n\n    for gen_idx in range(n_gens):\n        gen = generators[gen_idx]\n        if gen['gen_status'] == 0:\n            # Generator offline\n            x0.append(0.0)\n            bounds.append((0.0, 0.0))\n            gen_to_var[gen_idx] = {'pg': None, 'reserve': None}\n        else:\n            # Active generator\n            pg0 = gen['Pg'] if gen['Pg'] > 0 else (gen['Pmin'] + gen['Pmax']) / 2\n            x0.append(pg0)\n            bounds.append((gen['Pmin'], gen['Pmax']))\n\n            # Reserve is capacity coupling: reserve <= Pmax - Pg\n            max_reserve = gen['Pmax'] - pg0\n            x0.append(0.0)  # Initial reserve\n            bounds.append((0.0, max_reserve))\n\n            gen_to_var[gen_idx] = {\n                'pg': gen_idx,\n                'reserve': n_gens + gen_idx\n            }\n\n    # Build power balance constraint\n    # Sum of generation must equal load (plus losses approximated as 0 for DC power flow)\n    A_eq = np.zeros(n_vars)\n    for gen_idx in range(n_gens):\n        var_mapping = gen_to_var[gen_idx]\n        if var_mapping['pg'] is not None:\n            A_eq[var_mapping['pg']] = 1.0\n\n    b_eq = [total_load]\n\n    # Build reserve requirement constraint\n    A_neq = np.zeros(n_vars)\n    for gen_idx in range(n_gens):\n        var_mapping = gen_to_var[gen_idx]\n        if var_mapping['reserve'] is not None:\n            A_neq[var_mapping['reserve']] = 1.0\n\n    b_ineq = [reserve_requirement]\n\n    # Objective function\n    def objective(x):\n        cost = 0.0\n        for gen_idx in range(n_gens):\n            if gen_idx in gencosts and gen_to_var[gen_idx]['pg'] is not None:\n                pg_idx = gen_to_var[gen_idx]['pg']\n                pg = x[pg_idx]\n                coeffs = gencosts[gen_idx]['coeffs']\n                if len(coeffs) == 3:\n                    cost += coeffs[0] * pg**2 + coeffs[1] * pg + coeffs[2]\n                elif len(coeffs) == 2:\n                    cost += coeffs[0] * pg + coeffs[1]\n                elif len(coeffs) == 1:\n                    cost += coeffs[0] * pg\n        return cost\n\n    # Constraints\n    constraints = [\n        {'type': 'eq', 'fun': lambda x: A_eq @ x - b_eq[0]},\n        {'type': 'ineq', 'fun': lambda x: b_ineq[0] - A_neq @ x}\n    ]\n\n    # Solve optimization\n    print(f\"Solving optimization for {n_gens} generators, {n_buses} buses...\")\n    print(f\"Total load: {total_load:.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"Reserve requirement: {reserve_requirement:.2f} MW\")\n\n    result = minimize(\n        objective,\n        x0,\n        method='SLSQP',\n        bounds=bounds,\n        constraints=constraints,\n        options={'maxiter': 1000, 'ftol': 1e-9}\n    )\n\n    if not result.success:\n        print(f\"Warning: Optimization did not converge: {result.message}\")\n\n    # Extract results\n    dispatch_results = []\n    total_generation = 0.0\n    total_reserves = 0.0\n    total_cost = objective(result.x)\n\n    for gen_idx in range(n_gens):\n        gen = generators[gen_idx]\n        if gen_to_var[gen_idx]['pg'] is not None:\n            pg_idx = gen_to_var[gen_idx]['pg']\n            reserve_idx = gen_to_var[gen_idx]['reserve']\n\n            pg = result.x[pg_idx]\n            reserve = result.x[reserve_idx]\n\n            dispatch_results.append({\n                'id': gen_idx,\n                'bus': gen['bus'],\n                'output_MW': pg,\n                'reserve_MW': reserve,\n                'pmax_MW': gen['Pmax']\n            })\n\n            total_generation += pg\n            total_reserves += reserve\n        else:\n            # Offline generator\n            dispatch_results.append({\n                'id': gen_idx,\n                'bus': gen['bus'],\n                'output_MW': 0.0,\n                'reserve_MW': 0.0,\n                'pmax_MW': gen['Pmax']\n            })\n\n    # Calculate operating margin\n    total_capacity = sum(gen['Pmax'] for gen in generators.values() if gen['gen_status'] == 1)\n    operating_margin = total_capacity - total_generation - total_reserves\n\n    return {\n        'generator_dispatch': dispatch_results,\n        'totals': {\n            'cost_dollars_per_hour': total_cost,\n            'load_MW': total_load,\n            'generation_MW': total_generation,\n            'reserve_MW': total_reserves\n        },\n        'most_loaded_lines': most_loaded,\n        'operating_margin_MW': operating_margin\n    }\n\ndef calculate_line_flows(x: np.ndarray, generators: Dict, branches: Dict,\n                        buses: Dict, baseMVA: float) -> List[Dict]:\n    \"\"\"Calculate transmission line flows using DC power flow\"\"\"\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n\n    # Build admittance matrix\n    Y = build_y_matrix(branches, buses)\n\n    # Get generator outputs\n    pg_values = np.zeros(n_buses)\n    for gen_idx in range(n_gens):\n        gen = generators[gen_idx]\n        if gen['gen_status'] == 1:\n            pg_values[gen['bus'] - 1] = x[gen_idx]\n\n    # Build injection vector (generation - demand)\n    bus_injections = np.zeros(n_buses)\n    for bus_id, bus_data in buses.items():\n        bus_idx = bus_id - 1\n        bus_injections[bus_idx] = pg_values[bus_idx] - bus_data['Pd']\n\n    # Solve for voltage angles using DC power flow approximation\n    # B * theta = P_inj (for DC power flow, we use the imaginary part)\n    # For DC power flow, we approximate with the reactance matrix\n\n    # Build susceptance matrix (imaginary part of Y)\n    B = np.zeros((n_buses, n_buses))\n    for i in range(n_buses):\n        for j in range(n_buses):\n            if i != j:\n                B[i, j] = -Y[i, j].imag\n            else:\n                B[i, j] = sum(Y[i, k].imag for k in range(n_buses) if k != i)\n\n    # Reference bus is typically bus 1, remove it from the system\n    # For simplicity, assume bus 1 is the reference\n    B_reduced = B[1:, 1:]\n    P_reduced = bus_injections[1:]\n\n    try:\n        theta_reduced = np.linalg.solve(B_reduced, P_reduced)\n        theta = np.zeros(n_buses)\n        theta[1:] = theta_reduced\n        theta[0] = 0.0  # Reference bus\n    except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n        # If singular, use pseudo-inverse\n        theta = np.linalg.pinv(B) @ bus_injections\n        theta[0] = 0.0\n\n    # Calculate line flows\n    line_flows = []\n    for idx, branch in branches.items():\n        if branch['status'] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        from_bus = branch['from_bus'] - 1\n        to_bus = branch['to_bus'] - 1\n\n        # Power flow: P = (theta_from - theta_to) / x\n        x = branch['x']\n        if abs(x) > 1e-10:\n            flow = (theta[from_bus] - theta[to_bus]) / x\n        else:\n            flow = 0.0\n\n        # Calculate loading percentage\n        rating = branch['rateA'] if branch['rateA'] > 0 else 999999\n        loading_pct = abs(flow / rating) * 100\n\n        line_flows.append({\n            'from': branch['from_bus'],\n            'to': branch['to_bus'],\n            'loading_pct': loading_pct,\n            'flow_MW': abs(flow),\n            'rating_MVA': rating\n        })\n\n    return line_flows\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution\"\"\"\n    # Load network data\n    print(\"Loading network data...\")\n    network_data = load_network('/root/network.json')\n\n    # Solve economic dispatch\n    print(\"\\nSolving economic dispatch...\")\n    results = solve_economic_dispatch(network_data, reserve_percentage=0.05)\n\n    # For DC power flow, we use only reactance\n        x = branch['x']\n        if abs(x) < 1e-10:\n            continue\n\n        # Series admittance\n        y = 1.0j / x\n\n        # Add to Y matrix\n        Y[from_bus_idx, from_bus_idx] += y\n        Y[to_bus_idx, to_bus_idx] += y\n        Y[from_bus_idx, to_bus_idx] -= y\n        Y[to_bus_idx, from_bus_idx] -= y\n\n    return Y\n\ndef solve_economic_dispatch(network_data: Dict, reserve_percentage: float = 0.05) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve economic dispatch with spinning reserves using optimization\n\n    reserve_percentage: Spinning reserve requirement as percentage of load\n    \"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network_data['baseMVA']\n    buses = parse_bus_data(network_data['bus'])\n    generators = parse_gen_data(network_data['gen'])\n    gencosts = parse_gencost_data(network_data['gencost'])\n    branches = parse_branch_data(network_data['branch'])\n\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n\n    # Calculate total load\n    total_load = sum(bus['Pd'] for bus in buses.values())\n    reserve_requirement = reserve_percentage * total_load\n\n    # Prepare optimization variables\n    # Variables: [Pg1, Pg2, ..., Pgn, R1, R2, ..., Rn] for each generator\n    n_vars = 2 * n_gens\n\n    # Initial guess: start from current generation or midpoint of range\n    x0 = []\n    bounds = []\n\n    # Create mapping from generator index to variable index\n    gen_to_var = {}\n\n    for gen_idx in range(n_gens):\n        gen = generators[gen_idx]\n        if gen['gen_status'] == 0:\n            # Generator offline\n            x0.append(0.0)\n            bounds.append((0.0, 0.0))\n            gen_to_var[gen_idx] = {'pg': None, 'reserve': None}\n        else:\n            # Active generator\n            pg0 = gen['Pg'] if gen['Pg'] > 0 else (gen['Pmin'] + gen['Pmax']) / 2\n            x0.append(pg0)\n            bounds.append((gen['Pmin'], gen['Pmax']))\n\n            # Reserve is capacity coupling: reserve <= Pmax - Pg\n            max_reserve = gen['Pmax'] - pg0\n            x0.append(0.0)  # Initial reserve\n            bounds.append((0.0, max_reserve))\n\n            gen_to_var[gen_idx] = {\n                'pg': gen_idx,\n                'reserve': n_gens + gen_idx\n            }\n\n    # Build power balance constraint\n    # Sum of generation must equal load (plus losses approximated as 0 for DC power flow)\n    A_eq = np.zeros(n_vars)\n    for gen_idx in range(n_gens):\n        var_mapping = gen_to_var[gen_idx]\n        if var_mapping['pg'] is not None:\n            A_eq[var_mapping['pg']] = 1.0\n\n    b_eq = [total_load]\n\n    # Build reserve requirement constraint\n    A_neq = np.zeros(n_vars)\n    for gen_idx in range(n_gens):\n        var_mapping = gen_to_var[gen_idx]\n        if var_mapping['reserve'] is not None:\n            A_neq[var_mapping['reserve']] = 1.0\n\n    b_ineq = [reserve_requirement]\n\n    # Objective function\n    def objective(x):\n        cost = 0.0\n        for gen_idx in range(n_gens):\n            if gen_idx in gencosts and gen_to_var[gen_idx]['pg'] is not None:\n                pg_idx = gen_to_var[gen_idx]['pg']\n                pg = x[pg_idx]\n                coeffs = gencosts[gen_idx]['coeffs']\n                if len(coeffs) == 3:\n                    cost += coeffs[0] * pg**2 + coeffs[1] * pg + coeffs[2]\n                elif len(coeffs) == 2:\n                    cost += coeffs[0] * pg + coeffs[1]\n                elif len(coeffs) == 1:\n                    cost += coeffs[0] * pg\n        return cost\n\n    # Constraints\n    constraints = [\n        {'type': 'eq', 'fun': lambda x: A_eq @ x - b_eq[0]},\n        {'type': 'ineq', 'fun': lambda x: b_ineq[0] - A_neq @ x}\n    ]\n\n    # Solve optimization\n    print(f\"Solving optimization for {n_gens} generators, {n_buses} buses...\")\n    print(f\"Total load: {total_load:.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"Reserve requirement: {reserve_requirement:.2f} MW\")\n\n    result = minimize(\n        objective,\n        x0,\n        method='SLSQP',\n        bounds=bounds,\n        constraints=constraints,\n        options={'maxiter': 1000, 'ftol': 1e-9}\n    )\n\n    if not result.success:\n        print(f\"Warning: Optimization did not converge: {result.message}\")\n\n    # Extract results\n    dispatch_results = []\n    total_generation = 0.0\n    total_reserves = 0.0\n    total_cost = objective(result.x)\n\n    for gen_idx in range(n_gens):\n        gen = generators[gen_idx]\n        if gen_to_var[gen_idx]['pg'] is not None:\n            pg_idx = gen_to_var[gen_idx]['pg']\n            reserve_idx = gen_to_var[gen_idx]['reserve']\n\n            pg = result.x[pg_idx]\n            reserve = result.x[reserve_idx]\n\n            dispatch_results.append({\n                'id': gen_idx,\n                'bus': gen['bus'],\n                'output_MW': pg,\n                'reserve_MW': reserve,\n                'pmax_MW': gen['Pmax']\n            })\n\n            total_generation += pg\n            total_reserves += reserve\n        else:\n            # Offline generator\n            dispatch_results.append({\n                'id': gen_idx,\n                'bus': gen['bus'],\n                'output_MW': 0.0,\n                'reserve_MW': 0.0,\n                'pmax_MW': gen['Pmax']\n            })\n\n    # Calculate line flows using DC power flow\n    line_flows = calculate_line_flows(result.x, generators, branches, buses, baseMVA)\n\n    # Get most loaded lines\n    most_loaded = sorted(line_flows, key=lambda x: abs(x['loading_pct']), reverse=True)[:3]\n\n    # Calculate operating margin\n    total_capacity = sum(gen['Pmax'] for gen in generators.values() if gen['gen_status'] == 1)\n    operating_margin = total_capacity - total_generation - total_reserves\n\n    return {\n        'generator_dispatch': dispatch_results,\n        'totals': {\n            'cost_dollars_per_hour': total_cost,\n            'load_MW': total_load,\n            'generation_MW': total_generation,\n            'reserve_MW': total_reserves\n        },\n        'most_loaded_lines': most_loaded,\n        'operating_margin_MW': operating_margin\n    }\n\ndef calculate_line_flows(x: np.ndarray, generators: Dict, branches: Dict,\n                        buses: Dict, baseMVA: float) -> List[Dict]:\n    \"\"\"Calculate transmission line flows using DC power flow\"\"\"\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n\n    # Build admittance matrix\n    Y = build_y_matrix(branches, buses)\n\n    # Get generator outputs\n    pg_values = np.zeros(n_buses)\n    for gen_idx in range(n_gens):\n        gen = generators[gen_idx]\n        if gen['gen_status'] == 1:\n            pg_values[gen['bus'] - 1] = x[gen_idx]\n\n    # Build injection vector (generation - demand)\n    bus_injections = np.zeros(n_buses)\n    for bus_id, bus_data in buses.items():\n        bus_idx = bus_id - 1\n        bus_injections[bus_idx] = pg_values[bus_idx] - bus_data['Pd']\n\n    # Solve for voltage angles using DC power flow approximation\n    # B * theta = P_inj (for DC power flow, we use the imaginary part)\n    # For DC power flow, we approximate with the reactance matrix\n\n    # Build susceptance matrix (imaginary part of Y)\n    B = np.zeros((n_buses, n_buses))\n    for i in range(n_buses):\n        for j in range(n_buses):\n            if i != j:\n                B[i, j] = -Y[i, j].imag\n            else:\n                B[i, j] = sum(Y[i, k].imag for k in range(n_buses) if k != i)\n\n    # Reference bus is typically bus 1, remove it from the system\n    # For simplicity, assume bus 1 is the reference\n    B_reduced = B[1:, 1:]\n    P_reduced = bus_injections[1:]\n\n    try:\n        theta_reduced = np.linalg.solve(B_reduced, P_reduced)\n        theta = np.zeros(n_buses)\n        theta[1:] = theta_reduced\n        theta[0] = 0.0  # Reference bus\n    except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n        # If singular, use pseudo-inverse\n        theta = np.linalg.pinv(B) @ bus_injections\n        theta[0] = 0.0\n\n    # Calculate line flows\n    line_flows = []\n    for idx, branch in branches.items():\n        if branch['status'] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        from_bus = branch['from_bus'] - 1\n        to_bus = branch['to_bus'] - 1\n\n        # Power flow: P = (theta_from - theta_to) / x\n        x = branch['x']\n        if abs(x) > 1e-10:\n            flow = (theta[from_bus] - theta[to_bus]) / x\n        else:\n            flow = 0.0\n\n        # Calculate loading percentage\n        rating = branch['rateA'] if branch['rateA'] > 0 else 999999\n        loading_pct = abs(flow / rating) * 100\n\n        line_flows.append({\n            'from': branch['from_bus'],\n            'to': branch['to_bus'],\n            'loading_pct': loading_pct,\n            'flow_MW': abs(flow),\n            'rating_MVA': rating\n        })\n\n    return line_flows\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution\"\"\"\n    # Load network data\n    print(\"Loading network data...\")\n    network_data = load_network('/root/network.json')\n\n    # Solve economic dispatch\n    print(\"\\nSolving economic dispatch...\")\n    results = solve_economic_dispatch(network_data, reserve_percentage=0.05)\n\n    # Generate report\n    print(\"\\nGenerating report...\")\n    with open('/root/report.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(results, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(\"\\n=== DISPATCH SUMMARY ===\")\n    print(f\"Total Generation: {results['totals']['generation_MW']:.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"Total Load: {results['totals']['load_MW']:.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"Total Reserves: {results['totals']['reserve_MW']:.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"Total Cost: ${results['totals']['cost_dollars_per_hour']:.2f}/hr\")\n    print(f\"Operating Margin: {results['operating_margin_MW']:.2f} MW\")\n\n    print(\"\\n=== TOP 3 MOST LOADED LINES ===\")\n    for i, line in enumerate(results['most_loaded_lines'], 1):\n        print(f\"{i}. Line {line['from']} -> {line['to']}: {line['loading_pct']:.2f}% \"\n              f\"({line['flow_MW']:.2f} MW)\")\n\n    print(\"\\n=== GENERATOR DISPATCH ===\")\n    for gen in results['generator_dispatch']:\n        if gen['output_MW'] > 0:\n            print(f\"Gen {gen['id']} @ Bus {gen['bus']}: \"\n                  f\"{gen['output_MW']:.2f} MW, \"\n                  f\"{gen['reserve_MW']:.2f} MW reserves, \"\n                  f\"{gen['reserve_MW']/gen['pmax_MW']*100:.1f}%\")\n\n    print(\"\\nReport saved to report.json\")\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

Intermediate term rating (MVA)\n            'rateC': branch[7], # Short term rating (MVA)\n            'angle_min': branch[10], # Min angle difference (deg)\n            'angle_max': branch[11], # Max angle difference (deg)\n            'status': int(branch[9])  # 1=in service\n        }\n    return branches\n\ndef build_susceptance_matrix(branches: Dict, n_buses: int) -> np.ndarray:\n    \"\"\"Build susceptance matrix for DC power flow\"\"\"\n    B = np.zeros((n_buses, n_buses))\n\n    for idx, branch in branches.items():\n        if branch['status'] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        from_bus = branch['from_bus'] - 1\n        to_bus = branch['to_bus'] - 1\n        x = branch['x']\n\n        if abs(x) < 1e-10:\n            continue\n\n        b = 1.0 / x\n\n        B[from_bus, from_bus] += b\n        B[to_bus, to_bus] += b\n        B[from_bus, to_bus] -= b\n        B[to_bus, from_bus] -= b\n\n    return B\n\ndef solve_economic_dispatch(network_data: Dict, reserve_percentage: float = 0.05) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Solve economic dispatch with spinning reserves using optimization\n    \"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network_data['baseMVA']\n    buses = parse_bus_data(network_data['bus'])\n    generators = parse_gen_data(network_data['gen'])\n    gencosts = parse_gencost_data(network_data['gencost'])\n    branches = parse_branch_data(network_data['branch'])\n\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n\n    # Calculate total load\n    total_load = sum(bus['Pd'] for bus in buses.values())\n    reserve_requirement = reserve_percentage * total_load\n\n    # Get active generators\n    active_gens = [idx for idx in range(n_gens) if generators[idx]['gen_status'] == 1]\n    n_active = len(active_gens)\n\n    print(f\"Active generators: {n_active}\")\n    print(f\"Total load: {total_load:.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"Reserve requirement: {reserve_requirement:.2f} MW\")\n\n    # Variables: [Pg1, Pg2, ..., Pgn, R1, R2, ..., Rn] for active generators\n    x0 = []\n    bounds = []\n    var_map = {}  # Map from gen_idx to variable index\n\n    for i, gen_idx in enumerate(active_gens):\n        gen = generators[gen_idx]\n        pg0 = gen['Pg'] if gen['Pg'] > 0 else (gen['Pmin'] + gen['Pmax']) / 2\n        x0.append(pg0)\n        bounds.append((gen['Pmin'], gen['Pmax']))\n\n        max_reserve = gen['Pmax'] - pg0\n        x0.append(0.0)\n        bounds.append((0.0, max_reserve))\n\n        var_map[gen_idx] = {\n            'pg': 2 * i,\n            'reserve': 2 * i + 1\n        }\n\n    # Power balance: Sum Pg = Load\n    A_eq = np.zeros(2 * n_active)\n    for gen_idx in active_gens:\n        A_eq[var_map[gen_idx]['pg']] = 1.0\n\n    b_eq = total_load\n\n    # Reserve requirement: Sum R >= Reserve\n    A_neq = np.zeros(2 * n_active)\n    for gen_idx in active_gens:\n        A_neq[var_map[gen_idx]['reserve']] = 1.0\n\n    b_neq = reserve_requirement\n\n    # Objective function\n    def objective(x):\n        cost = 0.0\n        for gen_idx in active_gens:\n            if gen_idx in gencosts:\n                pg_idx = var_map[gen_idx]['pg']\n                pg = x[pg_idx]\n                coeffs = gencosts[gen_idx]['coeffs']\n                if len(coeffs) == 3:\n                    cost += coeffs[0] * pg**2 + coeffs[1] * pg + coeffs[2]\n                elif len(coeffs) == 2:\n                    cost += coeffs[0] * pg + coeffs[1]\n                elif len(coeffs) == 1:\n                    cost += coeffs[0] * pg\n        return cost\n\n    # Constraints\n    constraints = [\n        {'type': 'eq', 'fun': lambda x: A_eq @ x - b_eq},\n        {'type': 'ineq', 'fun': lambda x: A_neq @ x - b_neq}\n    ]\n\n    # Solve optimization\n    print(\"Solving optimization...\")\n\n    result = minimize(\n        objective,\n        x0,\n        method='L-BFGS-B',\n        bounds=bounds,\n        options={'maxiter': 500, 'ftol': 1e-6}\n    )\n\n    if not result.success:\n        print(f\"Warning: Optimization did not fully converge: {result.message}\")\n    else:\n        print(\"Optimization successful!\")\n\n    # Extract results\n    dispatch_results = []\n    total_generation = 0.0\n    total_reserves = 0.0\n    total_cost = objective(result.x)\n\n    for gen_idx in range(n_gens):\n        gen = generators[gen_idx]\n        if gen_idx in active_gens:\n            pg_idx = var_map[gen_idx]['pg']\n            reserve_idx = var_map[gen_idx]['reserve']\n\n            pg = result.x[pg_idx]\n            reserve = result.x[reserve_idx]\n\n            dispatch_results.append({\n                'id': gen_idx,\n                'bus': gen['bus'],\n                'output_MW': pg,\n                'reserve_MW': reserve,\n                'pmax_MW': gen['Pmax']\n            })\n\n            total_generation += pg\n            total_reserves += reserve\n        else:\n            # Offline generator\n            dispatch_results.append({\n                'id': gen_idx,\n                'bus': gen['bus'],\n                'output_MW': 0.0,\n                'reserve_MW': 0.0,\n                'pmax_MW': gen['Pmax']\n            })\n\n    # Calculate line flows using DC power flow\n    print(\"Calculating transmission line flows...\")\n    line_flows = calculate_line_flows(result.x, generators, active_gens, var_map,\n                                      branches, buses, baseMVA)\n\n    # Get most loaded lines\n    most_loaded = sorted(line_flows, key=lambda x: abs(x['loading_pct']), reverse=True)[:3]\n\n    # Calculate operating margin\n    total_capacity = sum(generators[idx]['Pmax'] for idx in active_gens)\n    operating_margin = total_capacity - total_generation - total_reserves\n\n    return {\n        'generator_dispatch': dispatch_results,\n        'totals': {\n            'cost_dollars_per_hour': total_cost,\n            'load_MW': total_load,\n            'generation_MW': total_generation,\n            'reserve_MW': total_reserves\n        },\n        'most_loaded_lines': most_loaded,\n        'operating_margin_MW': operating_margin\n    }\n\ndef calculate_line_flows(x: np.ndarray, generators: Dict, active_gens: List,\n                        var_map: Dict, branches: Dict, buses: Dict,\n                        baseMVA: float) -> List[Dict]:\n    \"\"\"Calculate transmission line flows using DC power flow\"\"\"\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n\n    # Build susceptance matrix\n    B = build_susceptance_matrix(branches, n_buses)\n\n    # Get generator outputs\n    pg_values = np.zeros(n_buses)\n    for gen_idx in active_gens:\n        gen = generators[gen_idx]\n        pg_idx = var_map[gen_idx]['pg']\n        pg_values[gen['bus'] - 1] = x[pg_idx]\n\n    # Build injection vector (generation - demand)\n    bus_injections = np.zeros(n_buses)\n    for bus_id, bus_data in buses.items():\n        bus_idx = bus_id - 1\n        bus_injections[bus_idx] = pg_values[bus_idx] - bus_data['Pd']\n\n    # Solve for voltage angles (DC power flow)\n    # Remove reference bus (bus 1, index 0)\n    B_reduced = B[1:, 1:]\n    P_reduced = bus_injections[1:]\n\n    try:\n        theta_reduced = np.linalg.solve(B_reduced, P_reduced)\n        theta = np.zeros(n_buses)\n        theta[1:] = theta_reduced\n        theta[0] = 0.0  # Reference bus\n    except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n        # If singular, use pseudo-inverse\n        theta = np.linalg.pinv(B) @ bus_injections\n        theta[0] = 0.0\n\n    # Calculate line flows\n    line_flows = []\n    for idx, branch in branches.items():\n        if branch['status'] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        from_bus = branch['from_bus'] - 1\n        to_bus = branch['to_bus'] - 1\n\n        x = branch['x']\n        if abs(x) > 1e-10:\n            flow = (theta[from_bus] - theta[to_bus]) / x\n        else:\n            flow = 0.0\n\n        # Calculate loading percentage\n        rating = branch['rateA'] if branch['rateA'] > 0 else 999999\n        loading_pct = abs(flow / rating) * 100 if rating > 0 else 0.0\n\n        line_flows.append({\n            'from': branch['from_bus'],\n            'to': branch['to_bus'],\n            'loading_pct': loading_pct,\n            'flow_MW': abs(flow),\n            'rating_MVA': rating\n        })\n\n    return line_flows\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution\"\"\"\n    # Load network data\n    print(\"Loading network data...\")\n    network_data = load_network('/root/network.json')\n\n    # Solve economic dispatch\n    print(\"\\nSolving economic dispatch...\")\n    results = solve_economic_dispatch(network_data, reserve_percentage=0.05)\n\n    # Generate report\n    print(\"\\nGenerating report...\")\n    with open('/root/report.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(results, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(\"\\n=== DISPATCH SUMMARY ===\")\n    print(f\"Total Generation: {results['totals']['generation_MW']:.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"Total Load: {results['totals']['load_MW']:.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"Total Reserves: {results['totals']['reserve_MW']:.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"Total Cost: ${results['totals']['cost_dollars_per_hour']:.2f}/hr\")\n    print(f\"Operating Margin: {results['operating_margin_MW']:.2f} MW\")\n\n    print(\"\\n=== TOP 3 MOST LOADED LINES ===\")\n    for i, line in enumerate(results['most_loaded_lines'], 1):\n        print(f\"{i}. matrix for DC power flow\"\"\"\n    B = np.zeros((n_buses, n_buses))\n\n    for idx, branch in branches.items():\n        if branch['status'] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        from_bus = branch['from_bus'] - 1\n        to_bus = branch['to_bus'] - 1\n        x = branch['x']\n\n        if abs(x) < 1e-10:\n            continue\n\n        b = 1.0 / x\n\n        B[from_bus, from_bus] += b\n        B[to_bus, to_bus] += b\n        B[from_bus, to_bus] -= b\n        B[to_bus, from_bus] -= b\n\n    return B\n\ndef solve_economic_dispatch(network_data: Dict, reserve_percentage: float = 0.05) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Solve economic dispatch with spinning reserves\"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network_data['baseMVA']\n    buses = parse_bus_data(network_data['bus'])\n    generators = parse_gen_data(network_data['gen'])\n    gencosts = parse_gencost_data(network_data['gencost'])\n    branches = parse_branch_data(network_data['branch'])\n\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    n_gens = np.ndarray:\n    \"\"\"Build susceptance matrix for DC power flow\"\"\"\n    B = np.zeros((n_buses, n_buses))\n\n    for idx, branch in branches.items():\n        if branch['status'] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        from_bus = branch['from_bus'] - 1\n        to_bus = branch['to_bus'] - 1\n        x = branch['x']\n\n        if abs(x) < 1e-10:\n            continue\n\n        b = 1.0 / x\n\n        B[from_bus, from_bus] += b\n        B[to_bus, to_bus] += b\n        B[from_bus, to_bus] -= b\n        B[to_bus, from_bus] -= b\n\n    return B\n\ndef calculate_generation_cost(pg: float, coeffs: List[float]) -> float:\n    \"\"\"Calculate generation cost given power output and cost coefficients\"\"\"\n    if len(coeffs) == 3:\n        return coeffs[0] * pg**2 + coeffs[1] * pg + coeffs[2]\n    elif len(coeffs) == 2:\n        return coeffs[0] * pg + coeffs[1]\n    elif len(coeffs) == 1:\n        return coeffs[0] * pg\n    else:\n        return pg  # Default linear cost\n\ndef solve_economic_dispatch(network_data: Dict, reserve_percentage: float = 0.05) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"Solve economic dispatch using direct calculation\"\"\"\n    baseMVA = network_data['baseMVA']\n    buses = parse_bus_data(network_data['bus'])\n    generators = parse_gen_data(network_data['gen'])\n    gencosts = parse_gencost_data(network_data['gencost'])\n    branches = parse_branch_data(network_data['branch'])\n\n    n_buses = len(buses)\n    n_gens = len(generators)\n\n    # Calculate total load\n    total_load = sum(bus['Pd'] for bus in buses.values())\n    reserve_requirement = reserve_percentage * total_load\n\n    # Get active generators\n    active_gens = [idx for idx in range(n_gens) if generators[idx]['gen_status'] == 1]\n\n    print(f\"Active generators: {len(active_gens)}\")\n    print(f\"Total load: {total_load:.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"Reserve requirement: {reserve_requirement:.2f} MW\")\n\n    # Calculate marginal costs for each generator\n    # For quadratic cost: C(P) = aP^2 + bP + c, marginal cost = 2aP + b\n    # For linear cost: C(P) = bP + c, marginal cost = b\n\n    generator_info = []\n    total_available_capacity = 0.0\n\n    for gen_idx in active_gens:\n        gen = generators[gen_idx]\n        if gen_idx in gencosts:\n            coeffs = gencosts[gen_idx]\n        else:\n            coeffs = [0.0, 1.0]  # Default $1/MW\n\n        # Available capacity (Pmax - current Pg if Pg is reasonable, otherwise Pmax - Pmin)\n        if gen['Pg'] > 0 and gen['Pg'] < gen['Pmax']:\n            available = gen['Pmax'] - gen['Pg']\n        else:\n            available = gen['Pmax'] - gen['Pmin']\n\n        total_available_capacity += available\n\n        # Calculate marginal cost at current operating point (use Pmin as starting point)\n        if len(coeffs) >= 2:\n            # Quadratic or linear with slope\n            a = coeffs[0] if len(coeffs) > 2 else 0.0\n            b = coeffs[1] if len(coeffs) > 1 else coeffs[0]\n            marginal_cost = 2 * a * gen['Pmin'] + b\n        else:\n            # Constant cost\n            marginal_cost = coeffs[0] if len(coeffs) > 0 else 1.0\n\n        generator_info.append({\n            'idx': gen_idx,\n            'bus': gen['bus'],\n            'pmax': gen['Pmax'],\n            'pmin': gen['Pmin'],\n            'pg': gen['Pg'],\n            'available': available,\n            'marginal_cost': marginal_cost,\n            'coeffs': coeffs\n        })\n\n    print(f\"Total available capacity: {total_available_capacity:.2f} MW\")\n\n    # Sort generators by marginal cost (merit order)\n    generator_info.sort(key=lambda g: g['marginal_cost'])\n\n    # Allocate generation based on merit order\n    remaining_load = total_load\n    dispatch_results = []\n    total_generation = 0.0\n    total_reserves = 0.0\n    total_cost = 0.0\n\n    for gen in generator_info:\n        # Allocate generation to meet remaining load\n        gen_max = min(gen['pmax'], gen['pmin'] + gen['available'])\n        gen_pg = max(gen['pmin'], min(gen_max, remaining_load))\n\n        # Allocate reserves - capacity coupling: R <= Pmax - Pg\n        max_reserve = gen['pmax'] - gen_pg\n        # Allocate proportional to available capacity\n        if total_available_capacity > 0:\n            reserve_share = gen['available'] / total_available_capacity\n            gen_reserve = min(max_reserve, reserve_share * reserve_requirement)\n        else:\n            gen_reserve = 0.0\n\n        dispatch_results.append({\n            'id': gen['idx'],\n            'bus': gen['bus'],\n            'output_MW': gen_pg,\n            'reserve_MW': gen_reserve,\n            'pmax_MW': gen['pmax']\n        })\n\n        total_generation += gen_pg\n        total_reserves += gen_reserve\n        total_cost += calculate_generation_cost(gen_pg, gen['coeffs'])\n\n        remaining_load -= gen_pg\n\n        if remaining_load <= 0:\n Economic Dispatch with Spinning Reserves (Final)\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nfrom typing import Dict, List\n\ndef load_network(filepath: str) -> Dict:\n    with open(filepath, 'r') as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n    return data\n\ndef parse_bus_data(bus_data: List) -> Dict:\n    buses = {}\n    for bus in bus_data:\n        bus_id = int(bus[0])\n        buses[bus_id] = {'Pd': bus[3]}\n    return buses\n\ndef parse_gen_data(gen_data: List) -> Dict:\n    generators = {}\n    for idx, gen in enumerate(gen_data):\n        bus_id = int(gen[0])\n        generators[idx] = {\n            'bus': bus_id,\n            'Pg': gen[1],\n            'Qmax': gen[3],\n            'Qmin': gen[4],\n            'Vg': gen[5],\n            'gen_status': int(gen[7]),\n            'Pmax': gen[8],\n            'Pmin': gen[9],\n        }\n    return generators\n\ndef parse_gencost_data(gencost_data: List) -> Dict:\n    costs = {}\n    for idx, cost in enumerate(gencost_data):\n        ncost = int(cost[3])\n        coeffs = cost[4:4+ncost]\n        costs[idx] = coeffs\n    return costs\n\ndef parse_branch_data(branch_data: List) -> Dict:\n    branches = {}\n    for idx, branch in enumerate(branch_data):\n        branches[idx] = {\n            'from_bus': int(branch[0]),\n            'to_bus': int(branch[1]),\n            'r': branch[2],\n            'x': branch[3],\n            'b': branch[4],\n            'rateA': branch[5],\n            'status': int(branch[9])\n        }\n    return branches\n\ndef build_susceptance_matrix(branches: Dict, n_buses: int) -> np.ndarray:\n    B = np.zeros((n_buses, n_buses))\n    for idx, branch in branches.items():\n        if branch['status'] == 0:\n            continue\n        from_bus = branch['from_bus'] - gen['output_MW'])\n        for gen in dispatch_results\n    )\n\n    print(f\"Total reserve capacity available: {total_reserve_capacity:.2f} MW\")\n\n    # Allocate reserves based on available capacity\n    for gen in dispatch_results:\n        gen_max_possible = generators[gen['id']]['Pmax']\n        available_for_reserve = max(0, gen_max_possible - 1] += gen['output_MW']\n\n    bus_injections = np.zeros(n_buses)\n    for bus_id, bus_data in buses.items():\n        bus_idx = bus_id - 1\n        bus_injections[bus_idx] = pg_values[bus_idx] - bus_data['Pd']\n\n    # DC power flow\n    B_reduced = B[1:, 1:]\n    P_reduced = bus_injections[1:]\n\n    try:\n        theta_reduced = np.linalg.solve(B_reduced, P_reduced)\n        theta = np.zeros(n_buses)\n        theta[1:] = theta_reduced\n        theta[0] = 0.0\n    except np.linalg.LinAlgError:\n        # Try pseudo-inverse\n        try:\n            theta = np.linalg.pinv(B) @ bus_injections\n            theta[0] = 0.0\n        except:\n            print(\"Warning: Could not solve DC power flow, using zero flows\")\n            theta = np.zeros(n_buses)\n\n    line_flows = []\n    for idx, branch in branches.items():\n        if branch['status'] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        from_bus = branch['from_bus'] - 1\n        to_bus = branch['to_bus'] - 1\n\n        x = branch['x']\n        if abs(x) > 1e-10:\n            flow = (theta[from_bus] - theta[to_bus]) / x\n        else:\n            flow = 0.0\n\n        rating = branch['rateA'] if branch['rateA'] > 0 else 999999\n        loading_pct = abs(flow / rating) * 100 if rating > 0 else 0.0\n\n        line_flows.append({\n            'from': branch['from_bus'],\n            'to': branch['to_bus'],\n            'loading_pct': loading_pct,\n            'flow_MW': abs(flow),\n            'rating_MVA': rating\n        })\n\n    return line_flows\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Loading network data...\")\n    network_data = load_network('/root/network.json')\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving economic dispatch...\")\n    results = solve_economic_dispatch(network_data, reserve_percentage=0.05)\n\n    print(\"\\nGenerating report...\")\n    with open('/root/report.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(results, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(\"\\n=== DISPATCH SUMMARY ===\")\n    print(f\"Total Generation: {results['totals']['generation_MW']:.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"Total Load: {results['totals']['load_MW']:.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"Power Balance Error: {abs(results['totals']['generation_MW'] -
